**错误码：** `"1107001"`

#### API-30 P-14 屈光质量指标（供 M22 / M17）

> **契约编号 P-14，路径与共享规范 §8 一字不差：** `GET /internal/v1/m11/quality/indicators`
> **调用方：** M22 报表决策、M17 成本核算 ｜ **SLA：** P95 ≤ 300ms

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m11/quality/indicators
    ?period_type=MONTH&period_key=2026-10
    &indicator_codes=EI_UCVA,SI_BCVA_LOSS,ST_REFRACT_CHANGE,PR_WITHIN_0_50,COMPLICATION_RATE
    &dimensions=ALL,REFRACTIVE_TYPE,DOCTOR&refractive_type=ALL&doctor_id=ALL
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period_type": "MONTH",
    "period_key": "2026-10",
    "indicator_version": "v3",
    "calc_batch_no": "ZB202610310100001",
    "calc_at": "2026-10-31T01:00:12",
    "surgery_eye_count": 138,
    "surgery_case_count": 69,
    "indicators": [
      {
        "indicator_code": "EI_UCVA",
        "indicator_name": "有效性指数",
        "category": "EFFICACY",
        "unit": "",
        "threshold_direction": "GREATER_OR_EQUAL_BETTER",
        "warn_threshold": 0.90,
        "alert_threshold": 0.80,
        "values": [
          { "dimension": "ALL", "dimension_value": "ALL", "value": 1.0210, "sample_count": 127, "expected_count": 134, "coverage_rate": 0.9478, "alert_level": "NORMAL", "available": true },
          { "dimension": "REFRACTIVE_TYPE", "dimension_value": "SMILE", "value": 1.0430, "sample_count": 62, "expected_count": 64, "coverage_rate": 0.9690, "alert_level": "NORMAL", "available": true },
          { "dimension": "REFRACTIVE_TYPE", "dimension_value": "FS_LASIK", "value": 1.0080, "sample_count": 41, "expected_count": 43, "coverage_rate": 0.9535, "alert_level": "NORMAL", "available": true },
          { "dimension": "REFRACTIVE_TYPE", "dimension_value": "TRANSPRK", "value": 0.8420, "sample_count": 24, "expected_count": 27, "coverage_rate": 0.8889, "alert_level": "ALERT", "available": true }
        ]
      },
      {
        "indicator_code": "SI_BCVA_LOSS",
        "indicator_name": "安全性指数",
        "category": "SAFETY",
        "unit": "行",
        "threshold_direction": "LESS_OR_EQUAL_BETTER",
        "warn_threshold": 0.10,
        "alert_threshold": 0.20,
        "values": [
          { "dimension": "ALL", "dimension_value": "ALL", "value": 0.0600, "sample_count": 127, "expected_count": 134, "coverage_rate": 0.9478, "alert_level": "NORMAL", "available": true }
        ]
      },
      {
        "indicator_code": "ST_REFRACT_CHANGE",
        "indicator_name": "稳定性",
        "category": "STABILITY",
        "unit": "D",
        "threshold_direction": "LESS_OR_EQUAL_BETTER",
        "warn_threshold": 0.25,
        "alert_threshold": 0.50,
        "values": [
          { "dimension": "ALL", "dimension_value": "ALL", "value": 0.1400, "sample_count": 121, "expected_count": 134, "coverage_rate": 0.9030, "alert_level": "NORMAL", "available": true }
        ]
      },
      {
        "indicator_code": "PR_WITHIN_0_50",
        "indicator_name": "可预测性",
        "category": "PREDICTABILITY",
        "unit": "",
        "threshold_direction": "GREATER_OR_EQUAL_BETTER",
        "warn_threshold": 0.80,
        "alert_threshold": 0.70,
        "values": [
          { "dimension": "ALL", "dimension_value": "ALL", "value": 0.9240, "sample_count": 127, "expected_count": 134, "coverage_rate": 0.9478, "alert_level": "NORMAL", "available": true }
        ]
      },
      {
        "indicator_code": "COMPLICATION_RATE",
        "indicator_name": "并发症率",
        "category": "SAFETY",
        "unit": "",
        "threshold_direction": "LESS_OR_EQUAL_BETTER",
        "warn_threshold": 0.03,
        "alert_threshold": 0.05,
        "values": [
          { "dimension": "ALL", "dimension_value": "ALL", "value": 0.0217, "sample_count": 138, "expected_count": 138, "coverage_rate": 1.0000, "alert_level": "WARNING", "available": true }
        ]
      }
    ],
    "doctor_values": [
      { "doctor_id": 2001, "doctor_name": "朱××", "surgery_eye_count": 46, "ei_ucva": 1.0410, "si_bcva_loss": 0.0410, "data_ref": "m11_quality_summary" }
    ],
    "availability": "AVAILABLE",
    "data_freshness": "REALTIME"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 只读，直接读 `m11_quality_summary`（命中 `uk_m11_quality_summary_key`），不实时计算 2) `indicator_codes` 为空时返回全部 8 个指标；`dimensions` 为空时返回 `ALL` 3) 每指标返回全部维度值数组，调用方可按需取用 4) `available=false` 时 `value` 为 null（分母为 0），**不返回 0** 5) 附带 `indicator_version` 与 `calc_batch_no` 供 M22 报表标注数据来源 6) `data_freshness`：当 `period_key` 为当前期间时返回 `REALTIME`（可能滞后至上次计算），否则 `FINALIZED` 7) 结果缓存 Redis `m11:quality:p14:{period_type}:{period_key}:{hash}` TTL 120s 8) P95 ≤ 300ms 由「单表索引扫描 + 缓存」双重保障。

**错误码：** `40001`、`"1107007"`、`"1107037"`

### 6.5 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m11/quality.py
"""M11-07 屈光质量与并发症管理 —— 路由层"""

from datetime import date
from typing import List, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Body, Depends, Header, Path, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import success_response
from app.core.security import require_permission
from app.schemas.m11.quality import (
    ComplicationAmendIn, ComplicationCreateIn, EventAdvanceIn,
    EventReportIn, IndicatorRecalcIn, IndicatorVersionIn,
    ReportGenerateIn, RetreatmentCreateIn, RetreatmentEvaluateIn,
)
from app.services.m11.quality_indicator_service import QualityIndicatorService
from app.services.m11.complication_service import ComplicationService
from app.services.m11.quality_event_service import QualityEventService
from app.services.m11.retreatment_service import RetreatmentService
from app.services.m11.quality_report_service import QualityReportService

# 对外路由：/api/v1/m11/quality
router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m11/quality", tags=["M11-07 屈光质量"])
# 内部契约：/internal/v1/m11/quality
internal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m11/quality",
                            tags=["M11-07 内部契约"])


# ==================== 质控主页 ====================

@router.get("/dashboard", summary="质控主页指标摘要")
async def quality_dashboard(
    period: str = Query(..., description="期间，如 2026-10"),
    refractive_type: str = Query("ALL"),
    eye_side: str = Query("ALL", pattern="^(OD|OS|OU|ALL)$"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回四大指标 + 并发症率 + 有效样本覆盖与排除构成。"""
    svc = QualityIndicatorService(db)
    data = await svc.dashboard(period=period, refractive_type=refractive_type,
                               eye_side=eye_side, user=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/dashboard/todo", summary="质控待办工作流汇总")
async def quality_todo(
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """按当前用户权限聚合待办，含超时标记与直达链接。"""
    svc = QualityEventService(db)
    data = await svc.todo_summary(user=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/dashboard/alerts", summary="质量告警列表")
async def quality_alerts(
    period: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    alert_level: str = Query("ALL"),
    unread_only: bool = Query(False),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """按级别与已读状态返回质量告警。"""
    svc = QualityIndicatorService(db)
    page_result = await svc.list_alerts(
        period=period, alert_level=alert_level, unread_only=unread_only,
        user=user, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    return success_response(page_result)


# ==================== 指标查询 ====================

@router.get("/indicators/summary", summary="指标多维明细查询")
async def indicator_summary(
    indicator_code: str = Query(..., description="指标编码，如 EI_UCVA"),
    period_type: str = Query("MONTH", pattern="^(DAY|MONTH|QUARTER|YEAR)$"),
    period_start: str = Query(...),
    period_end: str = Query(...),
    dimension: str = Query("ALL"),
    refractive_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    eye_side: str = Query("ALL"),
    sort_by: str = Query("alert_level"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回指标快照明细与口径元信息。"""
    svc = QualityIndicatorService(db)
    page_result = await svc.list_indicator_summary(
        indicator_code=indicator_code, period_type=period_type,
        period_start=period_start, period_end=period_end,
        dimension=dimension, refractive_type=refractive_type,
        eye_side=eye_side, sort_by=sort_by, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    return success_response(page_result)


@router.get("/indicators/trend", summary="指标趋势查询")
async def indicator_trend(
    indicator_code: str = Query(...),
    period_type: str = Query("MONTH"),
    period_start: str = Query(...),
    period_end: str = Query(...),
    dimension: str = Query("REFRACTIVE_TYPE"),
    refractive_types: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
):
    """返回按维度分组的指标时间序列与阈值。"""
    svc = QualityIndicatorService(db)
    data = await svc.trend(
        indicator_code=indicator_code, period_type=period_type,
        period_start=period_start, period_end=period_end,
        dimension=dimension, refractive_types=refractive_types,
    )
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/indicators/samples", summary="有效样本构成与排除明细")
async def indicator_samples(
    indicator_code: str = Query(...),
    period_key: str = Query(...),
    dimension: str = Query("ALL"),
    dimension_value: str = Query("ALL"),
    exclude_code: str = Query("ALL"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """回答「样本为什么少了」的唯一入口。"""
    svc = QualityIndicatorService(db)
    page_result = await svc.samples(
        indicator_code=indicator_code, period_key=period_key,
        dimension=dimension, dimension_value=dimension_value,
        exclude_code=exclude_code, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    return success_response(page_result)


@router.get("/indicators/{indicator_code}/versions", summary="指标口径版本历史")
async def indicator_versions(
    indicator_code: str = Path(...),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回该指标全部口径版本，回答「这个数怎么算的」。"""
    svc = QualityIndicatorService(db)
    page_result = await svc.list_versions(indicator_code, page, page_size)
    return success_response(page_result)


@router.get("/indicators/{indicator_code}/items", summary="指标病例明细下钻")
async def indicator_items(
    indicator_code: str = Path(...),
    period_key: str = Query(...),
    refractive_type: str = Query("ALL"),
    eye_side: str = Query("ALL"),
    is_valid: Optional[bool] = Query(None),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回可追溯的病例级分子分母值。"""
    svc = QualityIndicatorService(db)
    page_result = await svc.indicator_items(
        indicator_code=indicator_code, period_key=period_key,
        refractive_type=refractive_type, eye_side=eye_side,
        is_valid=is_valid, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    return success_response(page_result)


# ==================== 并发症 ====================

@router.get("/complications", summary="并发症分页列表")
async def list_complications(
    found_date_from: date = Query(...),
    found_date_to: date = Query(...),
    grade: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    complication_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    refractive_type: str = Query("ALL"),
    eye_side: str = Query("ALL"),
    doctor_id: Optional[int] = Query(None),
    report_status: str = Query("ALL"),
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """当前等级为 amend 回放后的有效值，同时返回原始等级与更正次数。"""
    svc = ComplicationService(db)
    page_result = await svc.list_complications(
        found_date_from=found_date_from, found_date_to=found_date_to,
        grade=grade, complication_type=complication_type,
        refractive_type=refractive_type, eye_side=eye_side,
        doctor_id=doctor_id, report_status=report_status, keyword=keyword,
        user=user, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    return success_response(page_result)


@router.get("/complications/{complication_no}", summary="并发症详情（含更正链）")
async def get_complication(
    complication_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """主档 + amend 回放得到当前有效视图，is_editable 恒为 false。"""
    svc = ComplicationService(db)
    data = await svc.get_detail(complication_no, user=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/complications", summary="登记并发症（只增）")
async def create_complication(
    payload: ComplicationCreateIn,
    request: Request,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:report")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """只执行 INSERT；需要上报时同事务创建不良事件。"""
    svc = ComplicationService(db)
    data = await svc.create(
        payload, operator=user, idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
        trace_id=request.state.trace_id,
    )
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/complications/{complication_no}/amend", summary="并发症更正（留痕）")
async def amend_complication(
    payload: ComplicationAmendIn,
    complication_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:grade")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """追加 amend 行，绝不 UPDATE 主档登记字段。"""
    svc = ComplicationService(db)
    data = await svc.amend(complication_no, payload, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/complications/{complication_no}/amends", summary="更正流水查询")
async def list_complication_amends(
    complication_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回完整更正链并校验 amend_seq 连续性。"""
    svc = ComplicationService(db)
    page_result = await svc.list_amends(complication_no, page, page_size)
    return success_response(page_result)


# ==================== 不良事件 ====================

@router.get("/events", summary="不良事件分页列表")
async def list_events(
    status: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    event_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    event_grade: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    overdue_only: bool = Query(False),
    doctor_id: Optional[int] = Query(None),
    date_from: date = Query(...),
    date_to: date = Query(...),
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:event")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回事件列表与按状态汇总。"""
    svc = QualityEventService(db)
    page_result = await svc.list_events(
        status=status, event_type=event_type, event_grade=event_grade,
        overdue_only=overdue_only, doctor_id=doctor_id,
        date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to, keyword=keyword,
        user=user, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    return success_response(page_result)


@router.get("/events/board", summary="不良事件闭环看板")
async def event_board(
    date_from: date = Query(...),
    date_to: date = Query(...),
    limit_per_column: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=50),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:event")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """按六态分列返回卡片与下一步动作提示。"""
    svc = QualityEventService(db)
    data = await svc.board(date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to,
                           limit_per_column=limit_per_column, user=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/events", summary="上报不良事件")
async def report_event(
    payload: EventReportIn,
    request: Request,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:event")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """GRADE_3/4 需后续 M20 审批；上报即计入及时率考核。"""
    svc = QualityEventService(db)
    data = await svc.report(payload, operator=user,
                            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                            trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/events/{event_no}/advance", summary="推进事件节点")
async def advance_event(
    payload: EventAdvanceIn,
    event_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:event")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """受理/定级/处置/复核/关闭，严格按六态状态机。"""
    svc = QualityEventService(db)
    data = await svc.advance(event_no, payload, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/events/{event_no}/void", summary="作废事件（误报）")
async def void_event(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    event_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:approve")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """需 M20 审批；不影响关联并发症主档。"""
    svc = QualityEventService(db)
    data = await svc.void(event_no, payload, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== 再手术与增效 ====================

@router.get("/retreatments", summary="再手术/增效申请列表")
async def list_retreatments(
    status: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    request_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    date_from: date = Query(...),
    date_to: date = Query(...),
    doctor_id: Optional[int] = Query(None),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回申请列表与按状态汇总。"""
    svc = RetreatmentService(db)
    page_result = await svc.list_retreatments(
        status=status, request_type=request_type, date_from=date_from,
        date_to=date_to, doctor_id=doctor_id, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    return success_response(page_result)


@router.get("/retreatments/eligibility", summary="适应症预校验（不落库）")
async def retreatment_eligibility(
    source_reservation_no: str = Query(..., max_length=32),
    eye_side: str = Query(..., pattern="^(OD|OS|OU)$"),
    request_type: str = Query(..., pattern="^(RE_TREATMENT|ENHANCEMENT)$"),
    planned_refractive_type: str = Query(...),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:retreat")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """实时汇总上游数据并逐条判定五项适应症标准。"""
    svc = RetreatmentService(db)
    data = await svc.check_eligibility(
        source_reservation_no=source_reservation_no, eye_side=eye_side,
        request_type=request_type,
        planned_refractive_type=planned_refractive_type,
    )
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/retreatments/{retreatment_no}", summary="申请详情（含适应症校验）")
async def get_retreatment(
    retreatment_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回申请详情与实时适应症校验结果。"""
    svc = RetreatmentService(db)
    data = await svc.get_detail(retreatment_no)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/retreatments", summary="新建再手术/增效申请")
async def create_retreatment(
    payload: RetreatmentCreateIn,
    request: Request,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:retreat")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """校验原方案已执行并跑适应症校验后落草稿。"""
    svc = RetreatmentService(db)
    data = await svc.create(payload, operator=user,
                            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                            trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/retreatments/{retreatment_no}/evaluate", summary="评估与批准")
async def evaluate_retreatment(
    payload: RetreatmentEvaluateIn,
    retreatment_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:approve")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """批准后可转 M11-04 建方案，失败时状态保持 APPROVED。"""
    svc = RetreatmentService(db)
    data = await svc.evaluate(retreatment_no, payload, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== 医师对比与报告 ====================

@router.get("/doctors/compare", summary="医师质量对比")
async def doctor_compare(
    period_start: str = Query(...),
    period_end: str = Query(...),
    refractive_type: str = Query("ALL"),
    min_sample: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=1000),
    sort_by: str = Query("complication_rate"),
    sort_order: str = Query("asc", pattern="^(asc|desc)$"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=200),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:view:all")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回医师矩阵、术式拆分与全院对照值。"""
    svc = QualityIndicatorService(db)
    page_result = await svc.doctor_compare(
        period_start=period_start, period_end=period_end,
        refractive_type=refractive_type, min_sample=min_sample,
        sort_by=sort_by, sort_order=sort_order,
        page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    return success_response(page_result)


@router.post("/indicators/recalc", summary="触发指标重算")
async def recalc_indicators(
    payload: IndicatorRecalcIn,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:config")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """生成新批次异步重算，旧批次保留可追溯。"""
    svc = QualityIndicatorService(db)
    data = await svc.trigger_recalc(payload, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/indicator-versions", summary="新增指标口径版本")
async def create_indicator_version(
    payload: IndicatorVersionIn,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:config")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """只追加新版本，历史版本口径定义不变。"""
    svc = QualityIndicatorService(db)
    data = await svc.create_version(payload, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/reports", summary="生成质量报告（异步）")
async def generate_report(
    payload: ReportGenerateIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:export")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """冻结计算批次后异步渲染 PDF/Excel。"""
    svc = QualityReportService(db)
    data = await svc.generate(payload, operator=user,
                              idempotency_key=idempotency_key)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/reports/{report_no}", summary="查询报告状态与下载地址")
async def get_report(
    report_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:quality:export")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回生成状态、文件列表与历史版本。"""
    svc = QualityReportService(db)
    data = await svc.get_detail(report_no)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== 内部契约（P-14 与并发症查询） ====================

@internal_router.get("/complications/{complication_no}",
                     summary="按并发症号取详情（内部）")
async def internal_get_complication(
    complication_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """供 M02 医疗安全核查，不含患者姓名与联系方式。"""
    svc = ComplicationService(db)
    data = await svc.get_internal(complication_no)
    return success_response(data)


@internal_router.get("/indicators", summary="P-14 屈光质量指标")
async def internal_quality_indicators(
    period_type: str = Query("MONTH", pattern="^(DAY|MONTH|QUARTER|YEAR)$"),
    period_key: str = Query(...),
    indicator_codes: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    dimensions: str = Query("ALL"),
    refractive_type: str = Query("ALL"),
    doctor_id: Optional[int] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """P-14：供 M22 / M17 拉取指标快照，只读，P95 ≤ 300ms。

    分母为 0 的指标 value 返回 null 而非 0，调用方须按 available 字段判断。
    """
    svc = QualityIndicatorService(db)
    data = await svc.p14_indicators(
        period_type=period_type, period_key=period_key,
        indicator_codes=indicator_codes, dimensions=dimensions,
        refractive_type=refractive_type, doctor_id=doctor_id,
    )
    return success_response(data)
```

### 6.6 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m11/complication_service.py
"""M11-07 并发症服务 —— 只增登记与更正留痕

设计铁律（医疗安全要求）：
  1. 并发症主档 m11_complication_ledger 只允许 INSERT，永不 UPDATE / DELETE
  2. 任何更正都通过追加 m11_complication_amend 行表达
  3. 读取时用「主档 + amend 按 seq 回放」得到当前有效视图
"""

import json
import logging
from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import and_, func, insert, select, text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m11.quality import (
    M11ComplicationAmend, M11ComplicationLedger, M11QualityEvent,
)
from app.schemas.m11.quality import ComplicationAmendIn, ComplicationCreateIn
from app.utils.idgen import next_no

logger = logging.getLogger("m11.quality.complication")

# 可更正字段白名单（其余字段一律不可更正）
AMENDABLE_FIELDS = {
    "complication_type",
    "complication_grade",
    "clinical_status",
    "found_date",
    "found_node",
    "symptoms",
    "sign_desc",
    "grade_basis",
    "disposition_detail",
}

# 强制上报的并发症类型（无论等级）
FORCE_REPORT_TYPES = {"INFECTION", "KERATECTASIA", "RETINA"}

# 13 类并发症类型（与共享规范 §13 一字不差）
VALID_TYPES = {
    "DLK", "HASE", "FLAP_COMPLICATION", "INFECTION", "KERATECTASIA",
    "REGRESSION", "UNDER_CORRECTION", "OVER_CORRECTION", "ASTIGMATISM",
    "GLAUCOMA", "CATARACT", "RETINA", "OTHER",
}

VALID_GRADES = {"GRADE_1", "GRADE_2", "GRADE_3", "GRADE_4"}


class ComplicationService:
    """并发症登记与更正。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession):
        self.db = db

    # ---------------- 登记（只增） ----------------

    async def create(self, payload: ComplicationCreateIn, operator: Any,
                     idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None,
                     trace_id: str = "") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """登记并发症：只执行 INSERT，不做任何 UPDATE。"""
        idem_key = idempotency_key or "COMPL:%s" % payload.reservation_no
        cached = await redis_client.get("m11:idem:complication:%s" % idem_key)
        if cached:
            return json.loads(cached)

        # ① 格式校验
        if payload.complication_type not in VALID_TYPES:
            raise BizException("1107008", "并发症类型非法：%s" % payload.complication_type)
        if payload.complication_grade not in VALID_GRADES:
            raise BizException("1107009", "并发症等级非法：%s" % payload.complication_grade)

        # ② 关联手术校验（只读引用 M11-05）
        surgery = await self._load_surgery(payload.reservation_no)
        if surgery is None or surgery.get("status") != "SURGERY_DONE":
            raise BizException("1107011", "关联手术不存在或未完成：%s" % payload.reservation_no)
        if payload.found_date < surgery["surgery_date"]:
            raise BizException("1107012", "发现日期 %s 早于手术日 %s"
                               % (payload.found_date, surgery["surgery_date"]))

        # ③ 文案完整性
        if len(payload.grade_basis or "") < 20:
            raise BizException("1107013", "分级依据不得少于 20 个字")
        if len(payload.sign_desc or "") < 10:
            raise BizException("1107013", "体征描述不得少于 10 个字")

        # ④ 系统建议等级 vs 医师判定
        suggested = self._suggest_grade(payload)
        overridden = suggested != payload.complication_grade
        if overridden and len(payload.grade_override_reason or "") < 10:
            raise BizException("1107014", "偏离系统建议等级 %s 必须填写理由" % suggested)

        # ⑤ 强制上报判定
        must_report = (payload.complication_grade in ("GRADE_3", "GRADE_4")
                       or payload.complication_type in FORCE_REPORT_TYPES)
        if must_report and not payload.report_to_event:
            raise BizException("1107015",
                               "该并发症（%s / %s）必须上报不良事件"
                               % (payload.complication_type, payload.complication_grade))

        now = datetime.now()
        created_nos: List[str] = []

        async with self.db.begin():
            # ⑥ 双眼拆两条
            eye_sides = ["OD", "OS"] if payload.eye_side == "OU" else [payload.eye_side]
            peer_map: Dict[str, str] = {}
            for eye in eye_sides:
                cno = await next_no("BZ")
                peer_map[eye] = cno
            for eye in eye_sides:
                peer = next((v for k, v in peer_map.items() if k != eye), None)
                ledger = M11ComplicationLedger(
                    complication_no=peer_map[eye],
                    reservation_no=payload.reservation_no,
                    plan_no=surgery["plan_no"],
                    patient_id=surgery["patient_id"],
                    eye_side=eye,
                    peer_complication_no=peer,
                    complication_type=payload.complication_type,
                    grade=payload.complication_grade,
                    system_suggested_grade=suggested,
                    grade_overridden=overridden,
                    grade_override_reason=payload.grade_override_reason,
                    found_date=payload.found_date,
                    found_node=payload.found_node,
                    found_by_type=payload.found_by_type,
                    found_dept=payload.found_dept,
                    followup_no=payload.followup_no,
                    symptoms=payload.symptoms,
                    sign_desc=payload.sign_desc,
                    ucva=payload.ucva,
                    bcva=payload.bcva,
                    iop_mmhg=payload.iop_mmhg,
                    slit_lamp=payload.slit_lamp,
                    grade_basis=payload.grade_basis,
                    disposition_measures=payload.disposition_measures,
                    disposition_detail=payload.disposition_detail,
                    next_visit_date=payload.next_visit_date,
                    registered_by=operator.id,
                    registered_at=now,
                )
                self.db.add(ledger)
                created_nos.append(peer_map[eye])

                # ⑦ 追加 REGISTER 流水（amend_seq=1）
                self.db.add(M11ComplicationAmend(
                    complication_no=peer_map[eye],
                    amend_seq=1,
                    action="REGISTER",
                    field_name=None,
                    old_value=None,
                    new_value=None,
                    amend_reason="首次登记",
                    amended_by=operator.id,
                    amended_at=now,
                ))

                # ⑧ 强制/自愿上报 → 同事务建事件
                event_no = None
                if payload.report_to_event:
                    event_no = await self._create_event(
                        complication_no=peer_map[eye],
                        grade=payload.complication_grade,
                        eye_side=eye,
                        reservation_no=payload.reservation_no,
                        patient_id=surgery["patient_id"],
                        found_at=payload.found_date,
                        reporter_id=operator.id,
                        summary=payload.sign_desc,
                    )
                if event_no:
                    self.db.add(M11ComplicationAmend(
                        complication_no=peer_map[eye],
                        amend_seq=2,
                        action="AMEND",
                        field_name="report_status",
                        old_value="PENDING",
                        new_value="REPORTED",
                        amend_reason="登记时按上报规则自动创建不良事件 %s" % event_no,
                        amended_by=operator.id,
                        amended_at=now,
                    ))

            await self.db.flush()

        result = {
            "complication_no": created_nos[0],
            "eye_side": payload.eye_side,
            "peer_complication_no": created_nos[1] if len(created_nos) > 1 else None,
            "complication_type": payload.complication_type,
            "complication_grade": payload.complication_grade,
            "system_suggested_grade": suggested,
            "grade_overridden": overridden,
            "clinical_status": "OPEN",
            "event_no": event_no,
            "registered_at": now.isoformat(),
        }
        await redis_client.setex("m11:idem:complication:%s" % idem_key, 86400,
                                 json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
        logger.info("complication registered nos=%s type=%s grade=%s trace=%s",
                    created_nos, payload.complication_type,
                    payload.complication_grade, trace_id)
        return result

    # ---------------- 更正（留痕） ----------------

    async def amend(self, complication_no: str, payload: ComplicationAmendIn,
                    operator: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """追加更正流水；绝不修改主档登记字段。"""
        ledger = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11ComplicationLedger).where(
                M11ComplicationLedger.complication_no == complication_no
            )
        )).scalars().first()
        if ledger is None:
            raise BizException("1107001", "并发症记录不存在：%s" % complication_no)

        current = await self._replay_current(complication_no)
        max_seq = (await self.db.execute(
            select(func.max(M11ComplicationAmend.amend_seq)).where(
                M11ComplicationAmend.complication_no == complication_no
            )
        )).scalar() or 0

        applied: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        need_event = False
        now = datetime.now()
        seq = max_seq

        async with self.db.begin():
            for item in payload.amends:
                if item.field_name not in AMENDABLE_FIELDS:
                    raise BizException("1107017",
                                       "字段不可更正：%s" % item.field_name)
                old_value = str(current.get(item.field_name) or "")
                if old_value == str(item.new_value):
                    raise BizException("1107018",
                                       "更正前后值相同：%s=%s"
                                       % (item.field_name, item.new_value))
                if len(item.amend_reason or "") < 10:
                    raise BizException("1107016", "更正原因不得少于 10 个字")

                if (item.field_name == "complication_grade"
                        and item.new_value in ("GRADE_3", "GRADE_4")
                        and not ledger.has_event):
                    need_event = True

                seq += 1
                self.db.add(M11ComplicationAmend(
                    complication_no=complication_no,
                    amend_seq=seq,
                    action="AMEND",
                    field_name=item.field_name,
                    old_value=old_value,
                    new_value=str(item.new_value),
                    amend_reason=item.amend_reason,
                    amended_by=operator.id,
                    amended_at=now,
                ))
                current[item.field_name] = item.new_value
                applied.append({
                    "amend_seq": seq,
                    "field_name": item.field_name,
                    "old_value": old_value,
                    "new_value": str(item.new_value),
                    "amended_by_name": operator.name,
                    "amended_at": now.isoformat(),
                })

            # 等级升级到 GRADE_3/4 时补建事件
            new_event_no = None
            if need_event:
                new_event_no = await self._create_event(
                    complication_no=complication_no,
                    grade=str(current.get("complication_grade")),
                    eye_side=str(ledger.eye_side),
                    reservation_no=ledger.reservation_no,
                    patient_id=ledger.patient_id,
                    found_at=ledger.found_date,
                    reporter_id=operator.id,
                    summary=str(current.get("sign_desc") or "")[:200],
                )

            await self.db.flush()

        await redis_client.delete("m11:quality:compl:%s" % complication_no)
        logger.info("complication amended no=%s fields=%s by=%s trace",
                    complication_no, [a["field_name"] for a in applied],
                    operator.id)
        return {
            "complication_no": complication_no,
            "amended_count": len(applied),
            "amend_records": applied,
            "current_grade": current.get("complication_grade"),
            "triggered_event_creation": need_event,
            "new_event_no": new_event_no,
            "original_record_untouched": True,
        }

    # ---------------- 内部辅助 ----------------

    async def _replay_current(self, complication_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """主档 + amend 按 seq 升序回放 = 当前有效视图。"""
        ledger = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11ComplicationLedger).where(
                M11ComplicationLedger.complication_no == complication_no
            )
        )).scalars().first()
        view: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "complication_type": ledger.complication_type,
            "complication_grade": ledger.grade,
            "clinical_status": "OPEN",
            "found_date": ledger.found_date,
            "found_node": ledger.found_node,
            "symptoms": ledger.symptoms,
            "sign_desc": ledger.sign_desc,
            "grade_basis": ledger.grade_basis,
            "disposition_detail": ledger.disposition_detail,
            "report_status": "PENDING",
        }
        amends = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11ComplicationAmend)
            .where(M11ComplicationAmend.complication_no == complication_no)
            .order_by(M11ComplicationAmend.amend_seq)
        )).scalars().all()
        for a in amends:
            if a.action == "AMEND" and a.field_name:
                view[a.field_name] = a.new_value
        return view

    @staticmethod
    def _suggest_grade(payload: ComplicationCreateIn) -> str:
        """按附录 B 规则给出建议等级（类型 + 干预 + 视力影响）。"""
        surgery_like = {"SURGERY_INTERVENE", "HOSPITALIZE"}
        measures = set(payload.disposition_measures or [])
        vision_lost = (payload.bcva is not None and payload.ucva is not None
                       and payload.bcva - payload.ucva >= 0.2)
        if payload.complication_type in ("INFECTION", "KERATECTASIA", "RETINA"):
            return "GRADE_4" if vision_lost else "GRADE_3"
        if measures & surgery_like:
            return "GRADE_3"
        if payload.complication_type in ("FLAP_COMPLICATION", "GLAUCOMA",
                                         "CATARACT") and vision_lost:
            return "GRADE_2"
        if payload.complication_type in ("DLK", "HASE") and vision_lost:
            return "GRADE_3"
        if measures:
            return "GRADE_2"
        return "GRADE_1"

    async def _create_event(self, complication_no: str, grade: str, eye_side: str,
                            reservation_no: str, patient_id: int, found_at: date,
                            reporter_id: int, summary: str) -> str:
        """在登记事务内创建不良事件（status=REPORTED）。"""
        event_no = await next_no("SJ")
        self.db.add(M11QualityEvent(
            event_no=event_no,
            event_type="COMPLICATION",
            complication_no=complication_no,
            patient_id=patient_id,
            eye_side=eye_side,
            reservation_no=reservation_no,
            event_grade=grade,
            status="REPORTED",
            found_at=datetime.combine(found_at, datetime.min.time()),
            reported_at=datetime.now(),
            reporter_id=reporter_id,
            event_summary=summary or "并发症登记自动创建事件",
            action_log=[{
                "seq": 1, "action": "REPORT",
                "at": datetime.now().isoformat(), "by": reporter_id,
            }],
        ))
        return event_no
```

```python
# app/services/m11/quality_indicator_service.py
"""M11-07 质量指标服务 —— 四类指标计算与 P-14 对外供给"""

import json
import logging
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import and_, select, text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m11.quality import (
    M11QualityIndicatorItem, M11QualityIndicatorVersion, M11QualitySummary,
)
from app.utils.idgen import next_no

logger = logging.getLogger("m11.quality.indicator")

# 指标编码 → 类别与阈值方向
INDICATOR_META = {
    "EI_UCVA": ("EFFICACY", "GREATER_OR_EQUAL_BETTER", 0.90, 0.80, ""),
    "EI_AMONG": ("EFFICACY", "GREATER_OR_EQUAL_BETTER", 0.80, 0.70, ""),
    "SI_BCVA_LOSS": ("SAFETY", "LESS_OR_EQUAL_BETTER", 0.10, 0.20, "行"),
    "SI_LOSS2": ("SAFETY", "LESS_OR_EQUAL_BETTER", 0.02, 0.05, ""),
    "ST_REFRACT_CHANGE": ("STABILITY", "LESS_OR_EQUAL_BETTER", 0.25, 0.50, "D"),
    "ST_SPH_CHANGE": ("STABILITY", "LESS_OR_EQUAL_BETTER", 0.25, 0.50, "D"),
    "PR_WITHIN_0_50": ("PREDICTABILITY", "GREATER_OR_EQUAL_BETTER", 0.80, 0.70, ""),
    "PR_WITHIN_1_00": ("PREDICTABILITY", "GREATER_OR_EQUAL_BETTER", 0.90, 0.85, ""),
}

# 排除码（对应 §3.4.5）
EXCLUDE_CODES = {
    "NO_BASELINE", "NO_POSTOP_DATA", "NO_TARGET", "OUT_OF_WINDOW",
    "TRANSFERRED_OUT", "POST_RETREATMENT", "EXTERNAL_SURGERY",
    "LOST_FOLLOWUP", "DATA_ANOMALY",
}


class QualityIndicatorService:
    """四大质量指标的计算、聚合与查询。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession):
        self.db = db

    # ---------------- 指标计算（核心） ----------------

    @staticmethod
    def calc_ucva_ratio(preop_bcva: Optional[Decimal],
                        postop_ucva: Optional[Decimal]) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """有效性单眼比值 = 术后 UCVA / 术前 BCVA；无数据返回 None。"""
        if not preop_bcva or not postop_ucva or preop_bcva <= 0 or postop_ucva <= 0:
            return None
        return (postop_ucva / preop_bcva).quantize(
            Decimal("0.0001"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

    @staticmethod
    def calc_logmar(va: Optional[Decimal]) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """小数视力 → logMAR；视力 ≤ 0 返回 None。"""
        if not va or va <= 0:
            return None
        import math
        return Decimal(str(-math.log10(float(va)))).quantize(
            Decimal("0.0001"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

    @classmethod
    def calc_bcva_loss_lines(cls, preop_bcva: Optional[Decimal],
                             postop_bcva: Optional[Decimal]) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """BCVA 下降行数 = logMAR(术前) − logMAR(术后)；提升计 0。"""
        lo_pre = cls.calc_logmar(preop_bcva)
        lo_post = cls.calc_logmar(postop_bcva)
        if lo_pre is None or lo_post is None:
            return None
        loss = lo_pre - lo_post
        return loss.quantize(Decimal("0.01"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP) \
            if loss > 0 else Decimal("0.00")

    @staticmethod
    def calc_se(sph: Optional[Decimal], cyl: Optional[Decimal]) -> Optional[Decimal]:
        """球镜等效 SE = sph + cyl / 2。"""
        if sph is None:
            return None
        c = cyl if cyl is not None else Decimal("0")
        return (sph + c / 2).quantize(Decimal("0.0001"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

    async def compute_period(self, period_type: str, period_key: str,
                             calc_batch_no: str,
                             dimension_list: List[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """计算指定期间的全部指标明细与快照。

        步骤：
          1) 拉取该期间符合条件的手术眼清单（含术前基线、术后数据）
          2) 逐眼逐指标计算 numerator / denominator，写明细
          3) 按各维度聚合写快照
          4) 与阈值比较写 alert_level，越界集合待告警任务发布
        """
        version = await self._current_version_map()
        rows = await self._fetch_cases(period_type, period_key)

        item_count = 0
        excluded_count = 0
        async with self.db.begin():
            for case in rows:
                exclude_code = self._judge_exclusion(case)
                if exclude_code:
                    excluded_count += 1
                for code in INDICATOR_META:
                    item = self._build_item(case, code, exclude_code,
                                            version.get(code), calc_batch_no)
                    if item is None:
                        continue
                    self.db.add(item)
                    item_count += 1
            await self.db.flush()

        summary_count = await self._build_summaries(
            period_type, period_key, dimension_list, calc_batch_no)
        logger.info("indicator computed period=%s items=%d excluded=%d summaries=%d",
                    period_key, item_count, excluded_count, summary_count)
        return {
            "period_key": period_key,
            "item_count": item_count,
            "excluded_count": excluded_count,
            "summary_count": summary_count,
            "calc_batch_no": calc_batch_no,
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _judge_exclusion(case: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[str]:
        """按 §3.4.5 判定该病例的排除原因；有效样本返回 None。"""
        if case.get("plan_source") == "OTHERS_HOSPITAL":
            return "EXTERNAL_SURGERY"
        if case.get("preop_bcva") is None:
            return "NO_BASELINE"
        if case.get("postop_ucva") is None:
            return "NO_POSTOP_DATA"
        if case.get("postop_ucva") is not None and (
                float(case["postop_ucva"]) > 2.0 or float(case["postop_ucva"]) < 0.01):
            return "DATA_ANOMALY"
        if case.get("out_of_window"):
            return "OUT_OF_WINDOW"
        if case.get("post_retreatment"):
            return "POST_RETREATMENT"
        if case.get("followup_status") == "MISSED" and case.get("postop_ucva") is None:
            return "LOST_FOLLOWUP"
        return None

    def _build_item(self, case: Dict[str, Any], code: str,
                    exclude_code: Optional[str], version: Optional[Any],
                    calc_batch_no: str) -> Optional[M11QualityIndicatorItem]:
        """构造单病例单指标明细行。"""
        preop_bcva = case.get("preop_bcva")
        postop_ucva = case.get("postop_ucva")
        postop_bcva = case.get("postop_bcva")
        preop_se = self.calc_se(case.get("preop_sph"), case.get("preop_cyl"))
        postop_se = self.calc_se(case.get("postop_sph"), case.get("postop_cyl"))
        target_se = case.get("target_se")

        numerator: Optional[Decimal] = None
        denominator: Optional[Decimal] = Decimal("1")

        if code in ("EI_UCVA", "EI_AMONG"):
            ratio = self.calc_ucva_ratio(preop_bcva, postop_ucva)
            if ratio is None:
                exclude_code = exclude_code or "NO_POSTOP_DATA"
            numerator = ratio
        elif code in ("SI_BCVA_LOSS", "SI_LOSS2"):
            numerator = self.calc_bcva_loss_lines(preop_bcva, postop_bcva)
            if numerator is None:
                exclude_code = exclude_code or "NO_POSTOP_DATA"
        elif code in ("ST_REFRACT_CHANGE", "ST_SPH_CHANGE"):
            numerator = case.get("se_change")
            if numerator is None:
                exclude_code = exclude_code or "OUT_OF_WINDOW"
        elif code in ("PR_WITHIN_0_50", "PR_WITHIN_1_00"):
            if target_se is None:
                exclude_code = exclude_code or "NO_TARGET"
            elif postop_se is None:
                exclude_code = exclude_code or "NO_POSTOP_DATA"
            else:
                err = abs(postop_se - Decimal(str(target_se)))
                threshold = Decimal("0.50") if code == "PR_WITHIN_0_50" else Decimal("1.00")
                numerator = Decimal("1") if err <= threshold else Decimal("0")

        if numerator is None and exclude_code is None:
            return None

        return M11QualityIndicatorItem(
            plan_no=case["plan_no"],
            reservation_no=case["reservation_no"],
            followup_no=case.get("followup_no"),
            patient_id=case["patient_id"],
            eye_side=case["eye_side"],
            node_code=case.get("node_code", "M1"),
            refractive_type=case["refractive_type"],
            doctor_id=case["doctor_id"],
            surgery_date=case["surgery_date"],
            indicator_code=code,
            indicator_version=version.version_no if version else "v1",
            numerator_value=numerator,
            denominator_value=denominator,
            is_valid=exclude_code is None,
            exclude_code=exclude_code,
            exclude_reason=self._exclude_reason_text(exclude_code),
            calc_batch_no=calc_batch_no,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _exclude_reason_text(code: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:
        """排除码 → 中文说明，写入明细供追溯。"""
        mapping = {
            "NO_BASELINE": "术前基线缺失（无 BCVA 或无屈光度）",
            "NO_POSTOP_DATA": "该节点术后数据缺失",
            "NO_TARGET": "手术方案无目标屈光度",
            "OUT_OF_WINDOW": "随访间隔超出节点窗口",
            "TRANSFERRED_OUT": "已转外院随访",
            "POST_RETREATMENT": "该节点前已做再手术/增效",
            "EXTERNAL_SURGERY": "外院术后转入",
            "LOST_FOLLOWUP": "失访",
            "DATA_ANOMALY": "数据类型异常，疑似录入错误",
        }
        return mapping.get(code) if code else None

    # ---------------- 聚合与阈值 ----------------

    async def _build_summaries(self, period_type: str, period_key: str,
                               dimension_list: List[str],
                               calc_batch_no: str) -> int:
        """按维度把明细聚合成快照，并写入 alert_level。"""
        count = 0
        for dimension in dimension_list:
            rows = await self._aggregate(period_type, period_key, dimension)
            for row in rows:
                meta = INDICATOR_META.get(row["indicator_code"])
                if meta is None:
                    continue
                _, direction, warn_th, alert_th, unit = meta
                value = row["value"]
                level = self._judge_level(value, direction, warn_th, alert_th)
                self.db.add(M11QualitySummary(
                    period_type=period_type,
                    period_key=period_key,
                    dimension=dimension,
                    dimension_value=row["dimension_value"],
                    indicator_code=row["indicator_code"],
                    indicator_version=row["indicator_version"],
                    indicator_value=value,
                    sample_count=row["sample_count"],
                    expected_count=row["expected_count"],
                    coverage_rate=row["coverage_rate"],
                    alert_level=level,
                    warn_threshold=Decimal(str(warn_th)),
                    alert_threshold=Decimal(str(alert_th)),
                    threshold_direction=direction,
                    unit=unit,
                    available=value is not None,
                    calc_batch_no=calc_batch_no,
                ))
                count += 1
        await self.db.flush()
        return count

    @staticmethod
    def _judge_level(value: Optional[Decimal], direction: str,
                     warn_th: float, alert_th: float) -> str:
        """阈值判定：分母为 0 时返回 NO_SAMPLE 而非误判。"""
        if value is None:
            return "NO_SAMPLE"
        v = float(value)
        if direction == "GREATER_OR_EQUAL_BETTER":
            if v < alert_th:
                return "ALERT"
            if v < warn_th:
                return "WARNING"
            return "NORMAL"
        if v > alert_th:
            return "ALERT"
        if v > warn_th:
            return "WARNING"
        return "NORMAL"

    # ---------------- P-14 对外供给 ----------------

    async def p14_indicators(self, period_type: str, period_key: str,
                             indicator_codes: Optional[str],
                             dimensions: str, refractive_type: str,
                             doctor_id: Optional[int]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """P-14：直接读快照表，不实时计算，P95 ≤ 300ms。"""
        cache_key = "m11:quality:p14:%s:%s:%s:%s:%s" % (
            period_type, period_key, indicator_codes or "ALL",
            dimensions, refractive_type)
        cached = await redis_client.get(cache_key)
        if cached:
            return json.loads(cached)

        codes = ([c.strip() for c in indicator_codes.split(",")]
                 if indicator_codes else list(INDICATOR_META.keys()))
        for c in codes:
            if c not in INDICATOR_META:
                raise BizException("1107007", "指标编码非法：%s" % c)

        dims = [d.strip() for d in dimensions.split(",")]
        rows = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11QualitySummary).where(and_(
                M11QualitySummary.period_type == period_type,
                M11QualitySummary.period_key == period_key,
                M11QualitySummary.indicator_code.in_(codes),
                M11QualitySummary.dimension.in_(dims),
            ))
        )).scalars().all()

        if not rows:
            raise BizException("1107042",
                               "期间 %s 无可用计算批次，请先执行指标计算" % period_key)

        grouped: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}
        batch_no = rows[0].calc_batch_no
        version = rows[0].indicator_version
        for r in rows:
            grouped.setdefault(r.indicator_code, []).append({
                "dimension": r.dimension,
                "dimension_value": r.dimension_value,
                "value": float(r.indicator_value) if r.indicator_value is not None else None,
                "sample_count": r.sample_count,
                "expected_count": r.expected_count,
                "coverage_rate": float(r.coverage_rate),
                "alert_level": r.alert_level,
                "available": r.available,
            })

        all_row = next((r for r in rows if r.dimension == "ALL"), rows[0])
        result = {
            "period_type": period_type,
            "period_key": period_key,
            "indicator_version": version,
            "calc_batch_no": batch_no,
            "calc_at": all_row.calc_at.isoformat() if all_row.calc_at else None,
            "surgery_eye_count": all_row.expected_count,
            "indicators": [
                {
                    "indicator_code": code,
                    "indicator_name": code,
                    "category": INDICATOR_META[code][0],
                    "unit": INDICATOR_META[code][4],
                    "threshold_direction": INDICATOR_META[code][1],
                    "warn_threshold": INDICATOR_META[code][2],
                    "alert_threshold": INDICATOR_META[code][3],
                    "values": grouped.get(code, []),
                }
                for code in codes
            ],
            "availability": "AVAILABLE",
            "data_freshness": "REALTIME",
        }
        await redis_client.setex(cache_key, 120,
                                 json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
        return result

    # ---------------- 数据加载（占位实现位于数据访问层） ----------------

    async def _fetch_cases(self, period_type: str,
                           period_key: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """从数据访问层拉取期间内的手术眼清单。

        实现位于 app/repositories/m11/quality_repo.py，
        通过 LEFT JOIN M11-06 随访与 M11-03 基线拼装以下字段：
        plan_no / reservation_no / patient_id / eye_side / node_code /
        refractive_type / doctor_id / surgery_date / plan_source /
        preop_bcva / postop_ucva / postop_bcva / preop_sph / preop_cyl /
        postop_sph / postop_cyl / target_se / se_change / out_of_window /
        post_retreatment / followup_status
        """
        from app.repositories.m11.quality_repo import fetch_period_cases
        return await fetch_period_cases(self.db, period_type, period_key)

    async def _aggregate(self, period_type: str, period_key: str,
                         dimension: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """按维度聚合明细；分母为 0 时 value 返回 None。"""
        from app.repositories.m11.quality_repo import aggregate_by_dimension
        return await aggregate_by_dimension(self.db, period_type,
                                            period_key, dimension)

    async def _current_version_map(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """取各指标当前生效口径版本。"""
        rows = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11QualityIndicatorVersion).where(
                M11QualityIndicatorVersion.effective_to.is_(None))
        )).scalars().all()
        return {r.indicator_code: r for r in rows}
```

---

## 7. 缓存与异步任务设计

### 7.1 Redis 缓存设计

| Key | 类型 | TTL | 说明 | 失效时机 | 容量估算 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m11:quality:dashboard:{period}:{type}:{eye}` | String(JSON) | 300s | 质控主页指标摘要 | 指标重算完成时按 period 删除 | 24 月 × 4 组合 ≈ 96 条 × 6KB ≈ 576KB |
| `m11:quality:p14:{period_type}:{period_key}:{hash}` | String(JSON) | 120s | **P-14 指标快照应答缓存** | 重算完成或口径变更时删除 | 12 期间 × 5 组合 ≈ 60 条 × 20KB ≈ 1.2MB |
| `m11:quality:summary:{period_key}:{dimension}` | Hash | 600s | 指标快照明细 | 该期间重算完成时删除 | 24 × 6 维度 ≈ 144 条 × 40KB ≈ 5.8MB |
| `m11:quality:compl:{complication_no}` | String(JSON) | 60s | 并发症回放视图（含 amend 链） | 该记录发生 amend 时删除 | 活跃记录约 200 条 × 4KB ≈ 800KB |
| `m11:quality:event:{event_no}` | String(JSON) | 30s | 事件详情 | 事件推进或作废时删除 | 未关闭约 60 条 × 3KB ≈ 180KB |
| `m11:quality:todo:{user_id}` | String(JSON) | 60s | 个人待办汇总 | 事件推进时按角色广播删除 `m11:quality:todo:*` | 20 用户 × 2KB ≈ 40KB |
| `m11:quality:alert:{period_key}` | Set | 3600s | 当期已发布告警去重集合 | 自然过期 | 24 期 × 30 成员 |
| `m11:quality:draft:{user_id}` | String(JSON) | 7200s | 并发症登记表单草稿 | 提交成功时删除 | 10 并发 × 8KB ≈ 80KB |
| `m11:quality:report:{report_no}` | String(JSON) | 1800s | 报告生成状态 | 报告完成或失败时更新 | 少量 |
| `m11:quality:version:{indicator_code}` | String | 3600s | 指标当前口径版本号 | 新增版本时删除 | 8 条 |
| `m11:idem:complication:{key}` | String(JSON) | 86400s | **登记幂等键** | 自然过期 | 470/月 × 2KB ≈ 1MB |
| `m11:idem:event:{key}` | String(JSON) | 86400s | **事件上报幂等键** | 自然过期 | 120/月 × 2KB ≈ 240KB |
| `m11:idem:retreatment:{key}` | String(JSON) | 86400s | **再手术申请幂等键** | 自然过期 | 20/月 × 2KB |
| `m11:idem:report:{key}` | String(JSON) | 86400s | **报告生成幂等键** | 自然过期 | 12/月 |
| `m11:code:{prefix}:{period}` | String | 至次日 03:05 | 单号计数器（BZ/SJ/ZS/ZB/ZLBG） | 每日 03:00 对账后过期 | 5 个 key |
| `m11:lock:quality:recalc:{period_key}` | String | 600s | 重算互斥锁 | 任务结束删除 | 极小 |
| `m11:lock:quality:report:{period_key}` | String | 900s | 报告生成互斥锁 | 任务结束删除 | 极小 |

**内存总量估算：** 峰值约 11 MB，其中指标快照缓存占主导（5.8MB）。

**缓存设计三原则：**

| # | 原则 | 本子模块的落地 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **指标快照必须可重建** | 所有指标缓存都从 `m11_quality_summary` 重建，Redis 完全丢失不影响正确性，只影响响应时间 |
| 2 | **并发症详情缓存须随 amend 失效** | 任何 amend 提交后立即删除 `m11:quality:compl:{no}`，避免读到旧回放结果（医疗安全事件不能看到过期数据） |
| 3 | **P-14 缓存 TTL 短于重算周期** | P-14 缓存 120s，重算完成主动删除；即使删除失败，最长 2 分钟后自动对齐 |

### 7.2 Celery 异步任务

| 任务名 | 触发时机 | 队列 | 重试策略 | 幂等 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `task_m11_quality_indicator_calc` | 随访完成事件 / 日终任务 | `m11_quality` | 最多 3 次，退避 60/300/900 秒 | 按 `(period_key, calc_batch_no)` |
| `task_m11_quality_alert_publish` | 重算完成且存在越界 | `m11_quality` | 最多 5 次，退避 30/120/600 秒 | 按 `(period_key, dimension, indicator_code, alert_level)` 24h 去重 |
| `task_m11_quality_report_generate` | API-27 触发 | `m11_report` | 最多 2 次，退避 120/600 秒 | 按 `report_no` + 版本 |
| `task_m11_quality_event_notify` | 事件上报/推进 | `m11_notify` | 最多 3 次，退避 30/120/600 秒 | 按 `(event_no, action, seq)` |
| `task_m11_quality_refresh_summary` | 单病例补录后 | `m11_quality` | 最多 3 次 | 按 `(period_key, dimension)` |
| `task_m11_quality_data_quality_scan` | 每日 02:30 | `m11_quality` | 不重试 | 按 `(scan_date)` |

```python
# app/tasks/m11/quality_tasks.py
"""M11-07 质量与并发症 —— Celery 异步任务"""

import json
import logging
from datetime import date, datetime

from celery import shared_task

from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client

logger = logging.getLogger("m11.quality")


@shared_task(
    name="m11.quality.indicator_calc",
    bind=True,
    max_retries=3,
    default_retry_delay=60,
    queue="m11_quality",
)
def task_m11_quality_indicator_calc(self, period_type: str, period_key: str,
                                    dimensions: list, calc_batch_no: str,
                                    trace_id: str = "") -> dict:
    """计算指定期间的四类质量指标（明细 + 快照 + 阈值判定）。

    幂等：同一 calc_batch_no 重复执行会先删除该批次已写入的明细行再重算，
    保证不会因重试产生重复明细。
    """
    from app.services.m11.quality_indicator_service import QualityIndicatorService

    try:
        with QualityIndicatorService.sync_session() as session:
            svc = QualityIndicatorService(session)
            svc.delete_batch_items(calc_batch_no)
            result = svc.compute_period_sync(
                period_type=period_type, period_key=period_key,
                calc_batch_no=calc_batch_no, dimension_list=dimensions,
            )
            svc.mark_batch_done(calc_batch_no, result["item_count"])

        for period_alias in (period_key,):
            redis_client.delete(
                "m11:quality:dashboard:%s:ALL:ALL" % period_alias)
            redis_client.delete_pattern("m11:quality:p14:%s:%s:*"
                                        % (period_type, period_alias))
            redis_client.delete_pattern("m11:quality:summary:%s:*" % period_alias)

        task_m11_quality_alert_publish.delay(period_type, period_key)
        logger.info("indicator calc done period=%s items=%d trace=%s",
                    period_key, result["item_count"], trace_id)
        return result
    except BizException as exc:
        logger.warning("indicator calc biz error code=%s msg=%s",
                       exc.code, exc.message)
        return {"ok": False, "code": exc.code}
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.error("indicator calc failed period=%s err=%s", period_key, exc)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc, countdown=60 * (2 ** self.request.retries))


@shared_task(name="m11.quality.alert_publish", bind=True, max_retries=5,
             queue="m11_quality")
def task_m11_quality_alert_publish(self, period_type: str,
                                   period_key: str) -> dict:
    """扫描越界指标并发布 m11.quality.alert（24 小时内同键去重）。"""
    from app.core.events import publish_event
    from app.services.m11.quality_indicator_service import QualityIndicatorService

    try:
        with QualityIndicatorService.sync_session() as session:
            svc = QualityIndicatorService(session)
            breaches = svc.find_breaches(period_key)

        published = 0
        for b in breaches:
            dedup_key = "m11:quality:alert:%s" % period_key
            member = "%s:%s:%s" % (b["dimension"], b["dimension_value"],
                                   b["indicator_code"])
            if not redis_client.sadd(dedup_key, member):
                continue
            redis_client.expire(dedup_key, 86400)
            publish_event(
                event_type="m11.quality.alert",
                payload={
                    "indicator_code": b["indicator_code"],
                    "indicator_name": b["indicator_name"],
                    "dimension": b["dimension"],
                    "dimension_value": b["dimension_value"],
                    "period_key": period_key,
                    "value": b["value"],
                    "warn_threshold": b["warn_threshold"],
                    "alert_threshold": b["alert_threshold"],
                    "alert_level": b["alert_level"],
                },
            )
            published += 1
        logger.info("alert published period=%s count=%d", period_key, published)
        return {"published": published}
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.error("alert publish failed period=%s err=%s", period_key, exc)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc)


@shared_task(name="m11.quality.report_generate", bind=True, max_retries=2,
             queue="m11_report")
def task_m11_quality_report_generate(self, report_no: str,
                                     trace_id: str = "") -> dict:
    """渲染质量报告（PDF / Excel），冻结计算批次数据。"""
    from app.services.m11.quality_report_service import QualityReportService

    lock_key = "m11:lock:quality:report:%s" % report_no
    if not redis_client.set(lock_key, "1", nx=True, ex=900):
        return {"skipped": True, "reason": "LOCK_HELD"}
    try:
        with QualityReportService.sync_session() as session:
            svc = QualityReportService(session)
            result = svc.render(report_no)
        logger.info("report generated no=%s pages=%s trace=%s",
                    report_no, result.get("page_count"), trace_id)
        return result
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.error("report generate failed no=%s err=%s", report_no, exc)
        with QualityReportService.sync_session() as session:
            QualityReportService(session).mark_failed(report_no, str(exc))
        raise self.retry(exc=exc, countdown=120 * (2 ** self.request.retries))
    finally:
        redis_client.delete(lock_key)


@shared_task(name="m11.quality.event_notify", bind=True, max_retries=3,
             queue="m11_notify")
def task_m11_quality_event_notify(self, event_no: str, action: str,
                                  trace_id: str = "") -> dict:
    """事件节点推进后通知相关角色（站内 + 短信）。

    注意：M11-07 不直接调 M03-05，通过发布事件由 M03-04 统一投递。
    """
    from app.core.events import publish_event

    try:
        publish_event(
            event_type="m11.quality.event.%s" % action.lower(),
            payload={"event_no": event_no, "action": action},
            trace_id=trace_id,
        )
        return {"ok": True, "event_no": event_no, "action": action}
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.error("event notify failed no=%s action=%s err=%s",
                     event_no, action, exc)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc)


@shared_task(name="m11.quality.data_quality_scan", queue="m11_quality")
def task_m11_quality_data_quality_scan(scan_date: str) -> dict:
    """每日数据质量扫描：找出无法进入有效样本的病例并归类。"""
    from app.services.m11.quality_indicator_service import QualityIndicatorService

    with QualityIndicatorService.sync_session() as session:
        svc = QualityIndicatorService(session)
        stats = svc.scan_data_quality(scan_date)
    logger.info("data quality scan date=%s stats=%s", scan_date, json.dumps(stats))
    return stats
```

### 7.3 APScheduler 定时任务

| 任务名 | Cron 表达式 | 说明 | 幂等与容错 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `job_m11_quality_indicator_daily` | `0 0 1 * * *`（每日 01:00） | 计算前一日指标明细与快照 | 按 `(period_key, calc_batch_no)` 幂等，重跑覆盖本批次 |
| `job_m11_quality_indicator_monthly` | `0 10 1 1 * *`（每月 1 日 01:10） | 计算上一整月指标（含 QUARTER/YEAR 维度） | 同上 |
| `job_m11_quality_alert_scan` | `0 30 1 * * *`（每日 01:30） | 扫描越界指标并发布告警 | 24 小时去重集合 |
| `job_m11_event_overdue` | `0 */10 * * * *`（每 10 分钟） | 刷新事件超时标记并升级 | 按状态阈值重算，幂等 |
| `job_m11_event_escalate` | `0 0 9 * * *`（每日 09:00） | 超时 2 倍阈值事件升级推送 | 按事件 + 日期去重 |
| `job_m11_quality_grade_check` | `0 0 * * * *`（每小时） | 检查 `GRADE_3/4` 并发症是否已建事件 | 缺失则补建事件并告警 |
| `job_m11_retreatment_followup` | `0 0 10 * * *`（每日 10:00） | 提醒待评估再手术/增效申请 | 按申请 + 日期去重 |
| `job_m11_quality_summary_repair` | `0 0 3 * * *`（每日 03:00） | 校验快照与明细加总一致性 | 不一致则标记并告警 |
| `job_m11_code_reconcile` | `30 0 3 * * *`（每日 03:30） | 单号计数器与数据库 max 对账 | 不一致则告警并重置 |
| `job_m11_quality_cache_warm` | `0 0 7 * * *`（每日 07:00） | 预热当期 P-14 与主页缓存 | 幂等（覆盖写） |

```python
# app/jobs/m11/quality_jobs.py
"""M11-07 质量与并发症 —— APScheduler 定时任务"""

import logging
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta

from apscheduler.schedulers.asyncio import AsyncIOScheduler
from sqlalchemy import and_, select, text

logger = logging.getLogger("m11.quality.jobs")


async def job_m11_event_overdue(session_factory) -> dict:
    """每 10 分钟：刷新不良事件超时状态并按阈值升级。

    超时阈值（与 §3.2.1 一致）：
      REPORTED       24h  未受理
      INVESTIGATING  72h  未定级
      GRADED         48h  未制定处置
      DISPOSING      30d  未完成处置
      REVIEWING      7d   未复核
    """
    thresholds = {
        "REPORTED": timedelta(hours=24),
        "INVESTIGATING": timedelta(hours=72),
        "GRADED": timedelta(hours=48),
        "DISPOSING": timedelta(days=30),
        "REVIEWING": timedelta(days=7),
    }
    updated = 0
    async with session_factory() as session:
        rows = (await session.execute(text("""
            SELECT event_no, status, status_changed_at
            FROM m11_quality_event
            WHERE status NOT IN ('CLOSED', 'VOIDED')
        """))).all()

        now = datetime.now()
        for event_no, status, changed_at in rows:
            limit = thresholds.get(status)
            if limit is None or changed_at is None:
                continue
            overdue = now - changed_at > limit
            await session.execute(
                text("""
                    UPDATE m11_quality_event
                    SET is_overdue = :od,
                        overdue_hours = :oh
                    WHERE event_no = :no
                """),
                {
                    "od": overdue,
                    "oh": int((now - changed_at).total_seconds() // 3600) if overdue else 0,
                    "no": event_no,
                },
            )
            if overdue:
                updated += 1
        await session.commit()

    logger.info("event overdue refresh updated=%d", updated)
    return {"overdue_updated": updated}


async def job_m11_quality_grade_check(session_factory) -> dict:
    """每小时：检查 GRADE_3/4 并发症是否已建事件，缺失则补建并告警。

    这是「强制上报」的第二道保险：登记时已校验，此处防御
    人工 amend 升级等级但漏建事件的情况。
    """
    created = 0
    async with session_factory() as session:
        rows = (await session.execute(text("""
            SELECT l.complication_no, l.complication_grade, l.reservation_no,
                   l.patient_id, l.eye_side, l.registered_by
            FROM v_m11_complication_current l
            WHERE l.current_grade IN ('GRADE_3', 'GRADE_4')
              AND NOT EXISTS (
                  SELECT 1 FROM m11_quality_event e
                  WHERE e.complication_no = l.complication_no
              )
        """))).all()

        for cno, grade, reservation_no, patient_id, eye, reporter in rows:
            await session.execute(text("""
                INSERT INTO m11_quality_event
                    (event_no, event_type, complication_no, patient_id,
                     eye_side, reservation_no, event_grade, status,
                     found_at, reported_at, reporter_id, event_summary,
                     action_log)
                VALUES
                    (:no, 'COMPLICATION', :cno, :pid, :eye, :res,
                     :grade, 'REPORTED', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                     CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, :reporter,
                     '系统巡检补建：重度并发症缺少关联事件', '[]'::jsonb)
            """), {
                "no": "SJ%s%04d" % (datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"), created + 1),
                "cno": cno, "pid": patient_id, "eye": eye,
                "res": reservation_no, "grade": grade,
                "reporter": reporter,
            })
            created += 1
        await session.commit()

    logger.info("grade check created_events=%d", created)
    return {"created_events": created}


async def job_m11_quality_indicator_daily(session_factory) -> dict:
    """每日 01:00：计算前一日指标，并触发告警扫描。"""
    from app.services.m11.quality_indicator_service import QualityIndicatorService

    target = date.today() - timedelta(days=1)
    period_key = target.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    batch_no = "ZB%s%03d" % (datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d%H%M"), 1)
    async with session_factory() as session:
        svc = QualityIndicatorService(session)
        result = await svc.compute_period(
            period_type="DAY", period_key=period_key,
            calc_batch_no=batch_no,
            dimension_list=["ALL", "REFRACTIVE_TYPE", "DOCTOR"],
        )
    logger.info("daily indicator computed date=%s result=%s",
                period_key, result)
    return result


def register_jobs(scheduler: AsyncIOScheduler, session_factory) -> None:
    """注册全部 M11-07 定时任务。"""
    scheduler.add_job(job_m11_quality_indicator_daily, "cron", hour=1, minute=0,
                      args=[session_factory], id="m11_quality_daily",
                      max_instances=1, coalesce=True)
    scheduler.add_job(job_m11_event_overdue, "cron", minute="*/10",
                      args=[session_factory], id="m11_event_overdue",
                      max_instances=1, coalesce=True)
    scheduler.add_job(job_m11_quality_grade_check, "cron", minute=0,
                      args=[session_factory], id="m11_grade_check",
                      max_instances=1, coalesce=True)
```

---

## 8. 业务规则与校验

### 8.1 编码规则

| 规则编号 | 规则 | 违反时返回 |
|---|---|---|
| R-8.1-01 | 并发症号 `BZ` + YYYYMMDD + 4 位，Redis INCR 生成 | `40001` |
| R-8.1-02 | 不良事件号 `SJ` + YYYYMMDD + 4 位 | `40001` |
| R-8.1-03 | 再手术/增效申请号 `ZS` + YYYYMMDD + 4 位 | `40001` |
| R-8.1-04 | 指标计算批次号 `ZB` + YYYYMMDDHHMM + 3 位 | `40001` |
| R-8.1-05 | 质量报告号 `ZLBG` + YYYYMM + 3 位 | `40001` |
| R-8.1-06 | 指标编码必须在 8 个标准编码内 | `"1107007"` |
| R-8.1-07 | 并发症类型必须在 13 类内（共享规范 §13） | `"1107008"` |
| R-8.1-08 | 并发症等级必须在 `GRADE_1`~`GRADE_4` 内 | `"1107009"` |
| R-8.1-09 | 术式码必须用 M01-07 的 10 个术式码，不自创 | `40001` |
| R-8.1-10 | 单号每日 03:30 与数据库 max 对账 | 告警并重置计数器 |
| R-8.1-11 | 单号永不重用（作废也不回收） | `40900` |

### 8.2 唯一性规则

| 规则编号 | 唯一约束 | 违反时返回 |
|---|---|---|
| R-8.2-01 | `m11_complication_ledger.complication_no` 唯一 | `40900` |
| R-8.2-02 | `m11_complication_amend (complication_no, amend_seq)` 唯一 | `40900` |
| R-8.2-03 | `m11_quality_event.event_no` 唯一 | `40900` |
| R-8.2-04 | `(complication_no, event_type)` 在未作废事件时唯一 | 返回已有事件号 |
| R-8.2-05 | `m11_quality_retreatment.retreatment_no` 唯一 | `40900` |
| R-8.2-06 | 同一原方案同眼同类型只允许一个进行中申请 | `40900` |
| R-8.2-07 | `m11_quality_indicator_version (indicator_code, version_no)` 唯一 | `40900` |
| R-8.2-08 | 同一指标生效期不得重叠 | `"1107039"` |
| R-8.2-09 | `m11_quality_indicator_item (plan_no, eye_side, node_code, indicator_code, calc_batch_no)` 唯一 | 重算前先删本批次 |
| R-8.2-10 | `m11_quality_summary (period_type, period_key, dimension, dimension_value, indicator_code, calc_batch_no)` 唯一 | 同上 |
| R-8.2-11 | 同一并发症 30 天内同眼同类型疑似重复 | 提示确认（不阻断） |
| R-8.2-12 | 幂等键 `m11:idem:{biz}:{key}` 24 小时内唯一 | 返回首次结果 |

### 8.3 状态流转规则

| 规则编号 | 规则 | 违反时返回 |
|---|---|---|
| R-8.3-01 | 不良事件只能按 §3.2.1 六态流转 | `"1107028"` |
| R-8.3-02 | `CLOSED` 与 `VOIDED` 为终态，任何操作拒绝 | `"1107027"` |
| R-8.3-03 | 复核人必须 ≠ 调查人且 ≠ 处置负责人（三方分离） | `"1107026"` |
| R-8.3-04 | 关闭前必须勾选「整改措施已落实」 | `"1107026"` |
| R-8.3-05 | `GRADE_3/4` 事件进入处置前必须有 M20 审批 | `"1107024"` |
| R-8.3-06 | 事件等级不得低于关联并发症等级 | `"1107048"` |
| R-8.3-07 | 作废仅限 `REPORTED`/`INVESTIGATING` | `"1107028"` |
| R-8.3-08 | **并发症主档无状态机**：登记即终态不可变，临床状态通过 amend 流水推进 | `"1107010"` |
| R-8.3-09 | 再手术/增效按 §3.2.3 五态流转 | `"1107035"` |
| R-8.3-10 | `COMPLETED`/`CANCELLED` 为终态 | `"1107035"` |
| R-8.3-11 | 转方案失败时状态保持 `APPROVED`，可重试 | `"1107036"` |
| R-8.3-12 | 指标计算批次状态 `RUNNING`→`DONE`/`FAILED`，不可回退 | `"1107041"` |

### 8.4 业务校验规则

**并发症登记校验：**

| 规则编号 | 校验 | 违反时返回 |
|---|---|---|
| R-8.4-01 | 关联手术必须存在且 `status='SURGERY_DONE'` | `"1107011"` |
| R-8.4-02 | 发现日期 ≥ 手术日且 ≤ 今天 | `"1107012"` |
| R-8.4-03 | 分级依据 ≥ 20 字 | `"1107013"` |
| R-8.4-04 | 体征描述 ≥ 10 字 | `"1107013"` |
| R-8.4-05 | 偏离系统建议等级必须写理由 ≥ 10 字 | `"1107014"` |
| R-8.4-06 | `GRADE_3/4` 或强制上报类型必须勾选上报 | `"1107015"` |
| R-8.4-07 | BCVA ≥ UCVA | `40001` |
| R-8.4-08 | 症状至少选 1 项 | `40001` |

**并发症更正校验：**

| 规则编号 | 校验 | 违反时返回 |
|---|---|---|
| R-8.4-09 | 更正字段必须在白名单内 | `"1107017"` |
| R-8.4-10 | 更正前后值不得相同 | `"1107018"` |
| R-8.4-11 | 更正原因 ≥ 10 字 | `"1107016"` |
| R-8.4-12 | 主档登记字段一律禁止 UPDATE/DELETE | `"1107010"` |
| R-8.4-13 | amend_seq 必须连续（发现断号视为数据损坏并告警） | 告警 |

**不良事件校验：**

| 规则编号 | 校验 | 违反时返回 |
|---|---|---|
| R-8.4-14 | 事件概述 ≥ 20 字 | `"1107020"` |
| R-8.4-15 | 调查人 ≠ 上报人 | `"1107021"` |
| R-8.4-16 | 调查报告 ≥ 50 字且含定级依据 | `"1107023"` |
| R-8.4-17 | 定级依据 ≥ 30 字 | `"1107023"` |
| R-8.4-18 | 处置结果与整改措施必填 | `"1107025"` |
| R-8.4-19 | 复核意见 ≥ 20 字 | `"1107026"` |
| R-8.4-20 | 作废原因 ≥ 10 字且需审批 | `"1107022"` |

**再手术/增效校验：**

| 规则编号 | 校验 | 违反时返回 |
|---|---|---|
| R-8.4-21 | 原方案必须已执行且有 `target_se` | `"1107030"` |
| R-8.4-22 | 申请依据 ≥ 30 字 | `"1107031"` |
| R-8.4-23 | `ENHANCEMENT` 术后 ≥ 3 个月 | `"1107032"` |
| R-8.4-24 | `ENHANCEMENT` 近两次 SE 变化 ≤ 0.50D | `"1107032"` |
| R-8.4-25 | 角膜厚度 ≥ 400μm 且 RSB ≥ 250μm | `"1107032"` |
| R-8.4-26 | 无活动性感染/膨隆/瓣并发症 | `"1107032"` |
| R-8.4-27 | 关联 `GRADE_3/4` 事件需 M20 审批 | `"1107034"` |
| R-8.4-28 | 驳回原因 ≥ 10 字 | `"1107033"` |

**指标与报告校验：**

| 规则编号 | 校验 | 违反时返回 |
|---|---|---|
| R-8.4-29 | 统计跨度 ≤ 24 个月 | `"1107037"` |
| R-8.4-30 | 重算原因 ≥ 10 字 | `"1107040"` |
| R-8.4-31 | 同期间同维度仅一个 `RUNNING` 批次 | `"1107041"` |
| R-8.4-32 | 报告章节至少 1 项 | `"1107043"` |
| R-8.4-33 | 报告必须先有计算批次 | `"1107042"` |
| R-8.4-34 | 指标值超出物理合理范围（EI > 3.0 或 < 0.1）标记异常 | `"1107046"` |

### 8.5 计算规则

| 规则编号 | 计算对象 | 公式 | 舍入 |
|---|---|---|---|
| R-8.5-01 | 有效性单眼比值 | `术后 UCVA / 术前 BCVA` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-02 | 有效性指数 EI | `Σ单眼比值 / 有效样本眼数` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-03 | 有效性达成率 | `UCVA ≥ BCVA 眼数 / 有效样本眼数` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-04 | logMAR 换算 | `logMAR = −log10(小数视力)` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-05 | BCVA 下降行数 | `logMAR(术前) − logMAR(术后)`，负值计 0 | 2 位小数 |
| R-8.5-06 | 安全性指数 SI | `Σ下降行数(仅正值) / 有效样本眼数` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-07 | 球镜等效 SE | `sph + cyl / 2` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-08 | 稳定性变化量 | `abs(SE后 − SE前)` 求和 ÷ 有效样本眼数 | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-09 | 可预测性误差 | `SE术后 − target_se` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-10 | 可预测性比例 | `abs(误差) ≤ 阈值 的眼数 / 有效样本眼数` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-11 | 并发症率 | `新登记并发症眼数 / 期间手术眼数` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-12 | 重度并发症率 | `GRADE_3+GRADE_4 眼数 / 期间手术眼数` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-13 | 再手术率 | `再手术眼数 / 期间手术眼数` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-14 | 有效样本覆盖率 | `有效样本眼数 / 应随访眼数` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-15 | 上报及时率 | `按时上报事件数 / 应上报事件数` | 4 位小数 |
| R-8.5-16 | 事件闭环时长 | `closed_at − reported_at`（小时） | 1 位小数 |
| R-8.5-17 | 综合评级 | 见附录 D 算法 | 等级 A/B/C/D |
| R-8.5-18 | 分母为 0 一律返回 NULL + `NO_SAMPLE` | 不返回 0，不返回 100% | — |

### 8.6 引用与删除规则

| 规则编号 | 规则 | 违反时返回 |
|---|---|---|
| R-8.6-01 | **并发症主档禁止物理 DELETE** | 数据库权限拒绝 |
| R-8.6-02 | **并发症更正流水禁止 UPDATE/DELETE** | 数据库权限拒绝 |
| R-8.6-03 | 不良事件禁止物理 DELETE，误报走 `VOIDED` | `"1107010"` |
| R-8.6-04 | 已关闭事件不可删除 | `"1107027"` |
| R-8.6-05 | 再手术申请禁止直接 DELETE，取消走 `CANCELLED` | `"1107010"` |
| R-8.6-06 | 指标口径版本禁止修改与删除，只能追加新版本 | `"1107010"` |
| R-8.6-07 | 指标明细与快照按批次整体删除（仅限同批次重算） | 仅系统任务可执行 |
| R-8.6-08 | 已生成报告不可修改，只能重新生成新版本 | `"1107010"` |
| R-8.6-09 | 所有表逻辑删除一律 `is_deleted=TRUE`（主档与流水表无此列） | 代码审查强制 |
| R-8.6-10 | 并发症关联的患者、手术、方案引用不做级联删除 | 应用层校验 |

### 8.7 并发控制

| 规则编号 | 场景 | 机制 | 冲突表现 |
|---|---|---|---|
| R-8.7-01 | 两人同时登记同一并发症 | `Idempotency-Key` + `uk(complication_no)` | 返回首次结果 |
| R-8.7-02 | 两人同时更正同一记录 | `amend_seq` 由 `MAX(seq)+1` 在事务内取，唯一约束兜底 | 后者 `40900` 并提示刷新 |
| R-8.7-03 | 两人同时推进同一事件节点 | 乐观锁 `version` + 状态机前置校验 | 后者 `"1107028"` |
| R-8.7-04 | 两人同时评估同一申请 | 乐观锁 `version` | 后者 `40900` |
| R-8.7-05 | 重算任务重复触发 | Redis 锁 `m11:lock:quality:recalc:{period_key}` | `"1107041"` |
| R-8.7-06 | 报告重复生成 | Redis 锁 + 幂等键 | `"1107044"` |
| R-8.7-07 | 告警重复发布 | Redis Set 24h 去重 | 静默跳过 |
| R-8.7-08 | 事件超时刷新并发 | `UPDATE ... WHERE event_no=:no` 条件更新 | 幂等无副作用 |
| R-8.7-09 | 口径版本并发新增 | 唯一约束 + 生效期重叠校验 | `"1107039"` |
| R-8.7-10 | 主档读取与 amend 写入并发 | `SELECT ... FOR SHARE` 读当前值 | 阻塞至 amend 提交 |

**加锁顺序规范（防死锁）：**

```
并发症域：按 (complication_no, amend_seq) 升序加锁
事件域： 按 event_no 单行加锁
指标域： 按 (calc_batch_no, item_id) 升序批量写入，不跨批次加锁
跨域：   先并发症主档，再事件表（amend 升级等级可能触发建事件）
```

---

## 9. 权限与安全

### 9.1 功能权限

| 权限编码 | 说明 | 授予角色 |
|---|---|---|
| `m11:quality:view` | 查看质控主页、指标看板、并发症与申请列表 | 质控医师、科主任、主刀医师（本人） |
| `m11:quality:view:all` | 查看全部医师质量数据与对比 | 科主任、医务科、质控办 |
| `m11:quality:report` | 登记并发症 | 主刀医师、随访医师、质控医师 |
| `m11:quality:grade` | 定级与更正并发症 | 质控医师、医务科 |
| `m11:quality:event` | 上报与推进不良事件 | 医务科、质控办、护士长、质控医师 |
| `m11:quality:approve` | 重大事件审批、事件作废、再手术评估批准 | 医务科、科主任 |
| `m11:quality:retreat` | 发起再手术/增效申请 | 主刀医师、质控医师 |
| `m11:quality:config` | 指标口径配置与重算 | 信息科、质控办 |
| `m11:quality:export` | 报告生成与导出 | 医务科、质控办、科主任 |
